    // Live Second Counter for Hero Countdown
    (function initDealTimers() {
      const heroSec = document.getElementById('hero-seconds');
      if (!heroSec) return;
      let seconds = 18;
      setInterval(() => {
        seconds = seconds > 0 ? seconds - 1 : 59;
        const formatted = seconds < 10 ? '0' + seconds : seconds;
        heroSec.innerHTML = `${formatted}<span class="text-[10px] font-normal block text-outline-variant -mt-1 uppercase text-center">sec</span>`;
      }, 1000);
    })();
